## DLC_APOCALYPSE_RECOMMENDATION_REASON_WARFARE_REC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADLC_APOCALYPSE_RECOMMENDATION_REASON_WARFARE_REC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们推荐§S《启示录》§!，因为该帝国的部分选定设置与其以战争为核心的机制和内容吻合。

### English

## TECH_UNLOCK_COLOSSUS_CONSTRUCTION_DESC

### 中文

§H巨像§!\n巨像比泰坦更加巨大，但舰体形式不适合船对船战斗。它更像是机动轨道站，只能够进行彻底的行星毁灭打击。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:56`

### English

§HColossus§!\nLarger than even a Titan, the Colossus hull configuration has no ship-to-ship combat capability. It is more of a mobile orbital station, capable only of complete planetary devastation.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:63`

## apoc.100.desc.pacif

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.100.desc.pacif&lang=en)

### 中文

随着威慑理论再次在[Root.Capital.GetName]上的政府学者们之间流行，巨像计划被认为是终极的威慑力量；这是一种甚至超越了泰坦级舰船的武器。尽管计划中的巨像可以携带完全摧毁整颗星球的武器，我们却希望能以更温和，但永久性的方式解决争端。\n\n假设敌人不愿停止对我们或是我们盟友的进攻，那我们就只好§R强迫他们停手§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:71`

### English

With deterrence theory once again in vogue among leading political scientists on [Root.Capital.GetName], the Colossus Project is presented as the ultimate deterrent; a weapon to surpass even the Titan ship class. While the proposed Colossus ships could carry payloads capable of complete planetary annihilation, we will deploy them using a less violent - but nevertheless terrifyingly permanent - method of conflict resolution.\n\nIf a hypothetical enemy will not cease their aggression toward us or our allies, we must simply §Rmake them§!.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:81`

## apoc.100.desc.milit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.100.desc.milit&lang=en)

### 中文

原本遮遮掩掩，而更早之前只在军工厂最隐蔽的角落里才被悄声提及的巨像计划，现在已经正式对外公布。这很可能是全星系史上最伟大的军事发明——巨像计划包含了开发§R歼星级§!武器平台，以及携带它的巨像舰体。因为除了计划中的行星终结者武器，容不下其它任何武装，巨像很可能需要护航，\n\n无论如何，一旦巨像成功部署，将会成为星系中最具破坏性的力量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:72`

### English

Previously spoken of only in hushed voices, and before then only in whispers in the most labyrinthine corridors of military skunkworks facilities, the Colossus Project now enjoys official sanction. Possibly the single greatest military innovation the galaxy will ever see, the Colossus Project comprises the development of a §RPlanet-Killer§! weapons platform as well as a Colossus hull configuration to carry it. The Colossus vessels will likely require an escort as the projected demands of a Planet-Killer weapon preclude any other armament.\n\nNevertheless, the Colossus, once developed, promises to be perhaps the single most destructive force in the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:82`

## apoc.100.desc.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.100.desc.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

巨像计划被誉为与荼毒星系的邪恶异星杂种们斗争中最重要的发明。正如设计的那样，巨像是一艘搭载着一门§R歼星级§!武器的重甲巨舰。因为异星杂种的威胁远未结束，它那能够轻易消灭星系中不愿屈服于我们的星球的能力，对我们来说必定将是无价之宝。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:73`

### English

The Colossus Project is being hailed as the single most important advancement in the ongoing struggle against the wretched aliens that plague the galaxy. As designed, a Colossus ship is a gargantuan, heavily armored frame fitted with a lone §RPlanet-Killer§!-class weapon. Its ability to simply wipe the galaxy clean of planets that will not submit to us will certainly prove invaluable, as the xeno threat is far from over.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:83`

## apoc.100.desc.purif

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.100.desc.purif&lang=en)

### 中文

以剑与火，燃异端之地。以彼之烬，沃泱泱星河。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:74`

### English

By sword and flame shall heretical worlds burn, and with their ashes shall the galaxy be salted.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:84`

## apoc.100.desc.termi

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.100.desc.termi&lang=en)

### 中文

大气层，生物群落，重力——虽然很便利——但只有可憎的血肉之躯才真正需要它们。\n\n摧毁这颗星球，摧毁这些有机体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:75`

### English

Atmospheres, biotopes, gravity - while frequently convenient - are only truly needed by the detestable meatbags.\n\nKill the planet, kill the organic.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:85`

## apoc.100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.100.a&lang=en)

### 中文

了解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:76`

### English

Acknowledged.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:86`

## apoc.110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

高悬穹顶的枪

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:77`

### English

The Gun Pointed at the Head of the Universe

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:87`

## apoc.110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

各个领域的著名科学家们终于一起敲定了装备在首批巨像级舰船上的§R歼星级§!武器的最终设计方案。我们能从中选择一种以完成巨像计划的最终阶段。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:78`

### English

Leading scientists across all conceivable fields have at last finalized their proposals for the §RPlanet-Killer§!-class weapon that will arm our first Colossus-type ships. We may select one proposal to develop alongside the final stages of the Colossus Project.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:88`

## apoc.110.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.110.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

现有的空闲处理循环确认足以开发一（1）种§R歼星级§!武器以适配§G巨像计划§!的竣工。\n\n需要授权优先级。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:79`

### English

Spare processing cycles have been estimated and deemed sufficient to develop one (1) §RPlanet-Killer§!-class weapon to coincide with completion of §GColossus Project§!.\n\nPrioritization needed.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:89`

## apoc.110.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.110.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

经过大量的试验，我们终于有能力研发出一套可以适配首批巨像级舰船的§R歼星级§!武器。其他的项目可以暂时被搁置，眼下我们只能将全部资源用于开发此武器，以便赶上巨像计划的收尾阶段。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:80`

### English

We have explored myriad possibilities, and it is now clear to us that we can develop a suitable §RPlanet-Killer§!-class weapon for our very first Colossus-type vessels. While other avenues can be pursued later, we can only spare the attention to develop one such weapon alongside the final stages of the Colossus Project.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:90`

## apoc.120.part.p1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.120.part.p1&lang=en)

### 中文

巨像计划——设计[Root.GetAdj]历史上最大武器平台、并搭载一门§R[Root.Get1stPlanetKiller]§!的里程碑式任务，已经取得成果，并且接近尾声。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:94`

### English

The Colossus Project, the monumental task of designing the biggest weapons platform in [Root.GetAdj] history and a §R[Root.Get1stPlanetKiller]§! to arm it with, has yielded results and drawn to a close.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:104`

## apoc.120.part.milit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.120.part.milit&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然第一艘巨像还没有建造出来，但这一刻是[Root.GetName]的历史的转折点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:95`

### English

While the first Colossus may not yet have been built, this very moment is the fulcrum upon which the history of the [Root.GetName] turns.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:105`

## apoc.120.part.pacif

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.120.part.pacif&lang=en)

### 中文

但愿我们永远不要走到这一步。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:96`

### English

Let us hope nothing will come of this.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:106`

## apoc.120.part.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.120.part.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

让外星杂种在我们的力量与才智面前颤抖吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:97`

### English

Let the xeno tremble in the face of our strength and ingenuity.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:107`

## apoc.120.part.mater

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.120.part.mater&lang=en)

### 中文

这将是[Root.GetAdj]创新能力的一次永恒见证。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:98`

### English

It will forever stand as a testament to [Root.GetAdj] innovation.

## apoc.120.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.120.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

巨像计划已完成。\n\n新舰船类型可用：\n<<§G巨像§!>>\n\n新武器部件可用：\n<<§R[Root.Get1stPlanetKiller]§!>>

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:99`

### English

Colossus Project completed.\n\nNew hull configuration available:\n<<§GColossus§!>>\n\nNew weapon component available:\n<<§R[Root.Get1stPlanetKiller]§!>>

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:109`

## apoc.120.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.120.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经完全掌握了巨像的构造，并且可以组装。而且，虽然不易，我们意识到梦魇般的§R[Root.Get1stPlanetKiller]§!也是可以被驾驭的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:100`

### English

The Colossus configuration has fully formed in our mind, ready to be assembled. And, with some unease, we realize that the §R[Root.Get1stPlanetKiller]§! nightmare can also be made manifest.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:110`

## tech_ftl_inhibitor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_ftl_inhibitor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

重力井投射器可以创造出局部无法安全进入超空间的区域。其能源需求与体积都十分巨大，只有空间站及以上等级才能安装。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:313`

### English

Gravity well projectors can be used to create localized pockets of space where safe entry into hyperspace is impossible. The immense power requirements and the size of the projectors prohibits their use on anything smaller than a space station.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:334`

## leviathans.340.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.340.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

太巧了！我们正打算提出和你说的基本类似的提议！如果你将此设施建设在与我们共享星系的[GetStarbaseOrWayStation]里，我们将派遣我们最优秀的艺术家前去当教师。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:320`

### English

Brilliant! We were just about to suggest the very same thing to you! If you establish this institution on the [GetStarbaseOrWayStation] in the system you share with us, we shall send some of our finest artists to serve there as teachers.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:342`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:343`

## action.82.name

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:401`

### English

[From.GetName] Bombarded

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:433`

## action.82.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.82.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一支§H[From.From.GetName]§!的舰队屠杀了§H[From.GetName]§!的居民！在一场毫不留情的大规模轨道轰炸后，地表上的所有人口聚集地都被彻底消灭了。[From.GetPlanetMoon]已经变成了一片充满辐射的废土，几乎无法支持生命。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:402`

### English

A fleet belonging to the §H[From.From.GetName]§! has massacred the population of §H[From.GetName]§!! After a massed and unrelenting orbital bombardment campaign, all population centers on the surface have been systematically wiped out, reducing the [From.GetPlanetMoon] to an irradiated wasteland, barely capable of supporting life.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:434`

## action.82.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.82.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这场暴行必须被血偿！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:403`

### English

This atrocity must be avenged!

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:435`

## action.83.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.83.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的军团报告称已完成对§H[From.GetName]§!的轰炸。所有人口聚集地都被彻底消灭了。现在充满辐射的[From.GetPlanetMoon]仅能支持极少的生命。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:404`

### English

Our forces report that they have concluded the orbital bombardment of §H[From.GetName]§!. All population centers have been systematically eradicated, and the irradiated [From.GetPlanetMoon] can now only support minimal life.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:436`

### 中文

这个轨道居住站被某种无法穿透的能量屏障封闭了。这个屏障屏蔽了对其地表进行的任何扫描。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:439`

### English

This Habitat station is encased in some kind of impenetrable energy barrier. It blocks all scans of its interior.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:475`

炸个球助兴

Cracking Open a Cold One

## planet_destruction.101.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.101.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着巨像完成其毁天灭地的任务，[From.From.GetName]因受到[From.GetName]地壳破裂与磁层分解的猛烈冲击而产生的微微晃动也平息了下来。\n\n我们很难理解一颗星球死亡时的那种壮烈，但[From.GetName]炽热的核心在冰冷宇宙中冷却时的那种死亡之美是无可争辩的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:443`

### English

The [From.From.GetName]'s gentle rocking - buffeted by [From.GetName]'s rupturing crust and unraveling magnetosphere - subsides as the Colossus completes its cataclysmic task.\n\nThe sheer violence of a dying planet is difficult to comprehend, but the funereal beauty of [From.GetName]'s molten core cooling in gelid space is undeniable.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:480`

## planet_destruction.101.a.purifexter

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.101.a.purifexter&lang=en)

### 中文

非常棒。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:444`

### English

Glorious.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:481`

## planet_destruction.301.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.301.name&lang=en)

### 中文

中子灭杀

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:445`

### English

Neutron Swept

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:483`

## planet_destruction.301.nobots.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.301.nobots.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]地表上的灭杀完成后，[From.From.GetName]上的中子羽流逐渐散去了。扫描显示未发现智慧生命迹象；植被虽被摧毁，但会逐渐恢复的。有些人会称这种彻底消灭智慧生命，只保留无机质的做法为邪恶的战争罪行。\n\n我们将其称为高效。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:446`

### English

The [From.From.GetName]'s neutron plume peters out, having finished its sweep of [From.GetName]'s surface. Scans report no signs of sapient life; plant life, while damaged, may recover in time. Some would call the total annihilation of intelligent life, while preserving inanimate matter, a perverse war crime.\n\nWe would call it efficient.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:484`

## planet_destruction.301.bots.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.301.bots.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]表面上的灭杀完成后，[From.From.GetName]上的中子羽流逐渐散去。\n\n声波传感器只传输来风声和合成人的呼声。所有高等有机生命已被扫除。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:447`

### English

The [From.From.GetName]'s neutron plume peters out, having finished its sweep of [From.GetName]'s surface.\n\nSonic sensors relay nothing but the wind and the gentle clicks and whirrs of the surviving synthetic populace. All sapient organic life has been wiped out.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:485`

## planet_destruction.301.infested.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.301.infested.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

完成了对[From.GetName]地表的灭杀之后，[From.From.GetName]的中子羽流渐渐消散。\n\n快速扫描确认了曾遍布地表的扭曲翻腾的有机质，已经彻底失去活性，仅剩贫瘠的石堆。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:448`

### English

The [From.From.GetName]'s neutron plume peters out, having finished its sweep of [From.GetName]'s surface.\n\nA quick scan confirms that the infested landscape, previously a roiling and writhing mass of organic activity, is now bereft of all life. Only barren rock remains.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:486`

## planet_destruction.401.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.401.name&lang=en)

### 中文

强制改信

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:449`

### English

Faith Enforced

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:488`

## planet_destruction.401.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.401.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.GetName]上的灵能的嗡声逐渐淡去，一瞬间万物寂静。[From.GetName]上的人群目瞪口呆（如果他们有眼和鼻的话），灵能射线从他们心智絮网中发散着，每个人都着迷于其强大和强烈的个人信仰。\n\n如果他们之前不明白的话，现在他们知道了：我们都只是某个更伟大事物的一部分。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:450`

### English

The empyreal hum of the [From.From.GetName]'s psionic coils fades, and for a moment all is quiet. Down on [From.GetName] the people stand slack-jawed (or local physiognomic equivalent), enraptured by the powerful and personal truths the psionic ray unfetters from the tangled web of their minds.\n\nIf they did not understand before, they know now viscerally: we are all part of something greater.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:489`

## planet_destruction.401.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.401.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

所有§Y$trait_mechanical$§!，§Y$trait_machine_unit$§!或§Y$trait_hive_mind$§!£pop£§H人口§!都被灵能共振§R摧毁§!。\n\n所有残存的£pop£§H生物人口§!变成§Y$ethic_spiritualist$§!。\n\n向[This.GetName]添加§Y$god_rayed$§!修正，持续§Y10年§!：\n唯心思潮吸引力：§Y+999%§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:451`

### English

Any §Y$trait_mechanical$§!, §Y$trait_machine_unit$§!, or §Y$trait_hive_mind$§! £pop£ §HPops§! are §Rdestroyed§! by the psionic resonance.\n\nAll remaining £pop£ §HBiological Pops§! become §Y$ethic_spiritualist$§!.\n\nAdds the §Y$god_rayed$§! modifier to [This.GetName] for §Y10 years§!:\nSpiritualist Ethics Attraction: §Y+999%§!.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:490`

## planet_destruction.501.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.501.name&lang=en)

### 中文

纳米灾疫散播

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:452`

### English

Nanobots Dispersed

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:492`

## planet_destruction.501.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.501.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.GetName]已完成纳米同化机器人的部署，其数量经计算足以义体化支配[From.GetName]上所有智慧有机生命。\n\n我们没有在[From.From.GetName]上布置任何传感器或器械以证明我们的成功，但我们知道已经成功了。当我们将目光投于[From.GetName]上时。我们见到了自己，也经由自己所见。\n\n在[From.GetName]表面上，只有我们自己。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:453`

### English

The [From.From.GetName] has completed deployment of the assimilation nanobot payload, calculated to be sufficient to cybernetically dominate all sapient organics on [From.GetName].\n\nWe did not build the [From.From.GetName] with any sensors or instruments to measure our success, but we know. We open our eyes for the first time down on [From.GetName]. We see ourselves and are seen by ourselves.\n\nOn [From.GetName]'s surface, there is only us.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:493`

## planet_destruction.501.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.501.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

所有£pop£§H有机人口§!获得§Y$trait_cybernetic$§!特质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:454`

### English

All £pop£ §HOrganic Pops§! gain the §Y$trait_cybernetic$§! trait.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:494`

## planet_destruction.701.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.701.name&lang=en)

### 中文

行星已被淹没

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:455`

### English

Planet Drenched

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:496`

## planet_destruction.701.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.701.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着从[FromFrom.GetName]上下降的水柱消散，[From.GetName]的表面完全改变了。曾经有广阔缺水的大陆，现在只有崭新闪耀着欢迎的微光的蓝色海洋，随时可以居住。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:456`

### English

As the column of water descending from the [FromFrom.GetName] dissipates, the surface of [From.GetName] is wholly transformed. Where once there were sprawling continents bereft of water, now there is only the welcoming shimmer of pristine, blue ocean ready to be inhabited.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:497`

## planet_destruction.701.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.701.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

星球变为§Y$pc_ocean$§!。\n没有§Y$trait_aquatic$§!特质的人口全部死亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:457`

### English

The planet becomes an §Y$pc_ocean$§!.\nAll Pops lacking the §Y$trait_aquatic$§! trait die.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:498`

## DELUGE_EXHAUSTED_ICE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADELUGE_EXHAUSTED_ICE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在§Y[This.GetName]§!的§Y天洪浩荡§!耗尽了我们位于$SYSTEM|Y$恒星系内的冰源，因此恒星系内的§Y$sm_ice_mining_station$§!已被拆除。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:459`

### English

Our use of the §YDeluge Machine§! on §Y[This.GetName]§! has exhausted our supply of ice in the $SYSTEM|Y$ system, so the §Y$sm_ice_mining_station$§! there has been disbanded.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:501`

## planet_destruction.801.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.801.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

来自[FromFrom.GetName]的滔天毒流渐渐平息，[From.GetName]上生意盎然的生态系统不复存在，充满毒气的星球中唯有少数几种生命形式能繁衍生息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:461`

### English

As the awesome stream of venomous substances from the [FromFrom.GetName] subsides, [From.GetName] is transformed from a vibrant ecosystem to a noxious world on which few forms of life can thrive.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:504`

## action.87.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.87.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对§Y[From.GetName]§!的管理控制已落入§Y[new_planet_owner.GetName]§!手里，因为他们控制了§Y[From.GetStarName]§!星系的§H恒星基地§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:479`

### English

We have lost administrative control of §Y[From.GetName]§! to §Y[new_planet_owner.GetName]§!, who controls the §HStarbase§! in the §Y[From.GetStarName]§! system.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:524`

## action.88.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.88.name&lang=en)

### 中文

获得殖民地！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:480`

### English

Colony Gained!

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:525`

## action.88.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.88.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已获得了§Y[From.GetName]§!的行政控制权，这此前是§Y[former_planet_owner.GetName]§!的一处据点，如今我们控制了§Y[From.GetStarName]§!星系的§H恒星基地§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:481`

### English

We have gained administrative control of §Y[From.GetName]§!, formerly a holding of §Y[former_planet_owner.GetName]§!, because we control the §HStarbase§! in the §Y[From.GetStarName]§! system.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:526`

## apoc.1.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.1.name&lang=en)

### 中文

遗弃星门

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:482`

### English

Abandoned Gateway

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:528`

## apoc.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了一处位于[gateway_system.GetName]星系边缘的远古空间站。其残留的强烈亚空间信号表明其可能是一个§H星门§!之类的东西——理论上能在类似的空间站间组成网状通路，使舰船能瞬移至星系的不同位置。\n\n假如它真是一个星门的话，现在也因为年久失修而无法使用。不过，如果我们能将其修复，星门拥有者将能激活星系中其他星门，使其能在短短数天穿越极远的距离。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:483`

### English

An ancient space station of some kind has been located at the edge of the [gateway_system.GetName] system. The strong residual subspace signature that surrounds the station indicates that it may be a "§HGateway§!" of sorts - part of a theoretical network of similar stations that at some point would have allowed for near-instantaneous travel between different parts of the galaxy.\n\nAssuming this is a Gateway, its current state of disrepair has rendered it unusable. If it could be restored into working order, its owner would potentially be able to access any other functional gateways in the galaxy, enabling travel across huge distances in a matter of days.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:529`

## apoc.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[gateway_system.GetName]星系又发现了另一座废弃的星门。与第一个一样，这个星门现在也由于年久失修而无法使用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:484`

### English

We have encountered another abandoned Gateway, this time in the [gateway_system.GetName] system. Just like the first one, this Gateway is also currently inoperable due to its poor condition.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:530`

## apoc.3.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.3.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在§H[gateway_system.GetName]§!星系附近发现了一座远古亚空间传送门。它似乎是一系列相似的星系网络站点的一部分，当它们可运作时，将允许使用者近乎瞬间地从星系的一处转移到另一处。\n\n在其初始建成之后的某个时间点，它与网络的其余部分间的连接被切断了。它现在只能指示出一个有效的目的地——一片位于我们星系外的未经探索的星域，其被我们的天文学家命名为§HL-星团§!。更耐人寻味的是，这座星门完全是由某种未知设计的微型§H纳米机器人§!建造的。\n\n虽然星门散发着微弱的能量特征，但它被故意锁定在某种维护循环中，以防止其被激活。除非我们能设法解除这一过程，不然我们将无法抵达L-星团。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:485`

### English

We have located what we believe could be an ancient subspace Gateway near §H[gateway_system.GetName]§!. It appears to have been part of a galaxy-wide network of similar stations which, when operational, allowed for near-instantaneous travel from one part of the galaxy to another.\n\nAt some point after its initial construction its connection to the rest of the network was severed. It now offers only one valid destination - an uncharted group of stars just beyond our galaxy that our astronomers have designated the §HL-Cluster§!. Even more intriguing, the Gateway was built entirely by microscopic §HNanites§! of an unknown design.\n\nAlthough the Gateway emits a faint power signature, it has been deliberately locked into some kind of maintenance loop that prevents its activation. Until we find some way of aborting this process, the L-Cluster will remain beyond our reach.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:531`

## apoc.5.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.5.name&lang=en)

### 中文

天然虫洞

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:486`

### English

Natural Wormhole

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:532`

## apoc.5.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.5.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在§H[wormhole_system.GetName]§!星系的边缘疑似发现了一个天然形成的亚空间现象！该处的时空出现了裂隙，从而产生了一个\"§H虫洞§!\"。据[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]推断，此虫洞也许与星系中的其它虫洞通过亚空间相连。\n\n这也就是说，我们的舰船有可能在数日之内就可以由星系的一端到达另一端，这取决于另外的虫洞的位置。不幸的是这个虫洞和大部分虫洞一样，长时间处于不稳定状态。即使有舰船敢接近，也会瞬间被撕成碎片。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:487`

### English

We have detected what appears to be a naturally occurring subspace phenomenon on the edge of the §H[wormhole_system.GetName]§! system! A rift in the very fabric of space-time has formed here, creating a "§Hwormhole§!" that, our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] speculate, may provide a conduit through subspace to another wormhole located somewhere else in our galaxy.\n\nDepending on where the second wormhole is located, this could potentially allow ships to travel from one end of the galaxy to the other in a matter of days. Unfortunately, this wormhole, like the vast majority of its kind, is inherently unstable. Any vessels foolish enough to pass through it would be ripped apart in seconds.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:533`

## apoc.5.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.5.a&lang=en)

### 中文

如果我们能将其稳定的话……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:488`

### English

If it could somehow be stabilized...

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:534`

## apoc.10.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.10.name&lang=en)

### 中文

重新激活星门

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:489`

### English

Gateway Reactivated

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:535`

## apoc.10.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.10.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

$apoc.10.desc.a$\n\n$apoc.10.desc.b$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:490`

### English

$apoc.10.desc.a$\n\n$apoc.10.desc.b$

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:536`

## apoc.10.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.10.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

$apoc.10.desc.a$\n\n$apoc.10.desc.c$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:491`

### English

$apoc.10.desc.a$\n\n$apoc.10.desc.c$

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:537`

## apoc.10.desc.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.10.desc.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的科学家已成功恢复了了位于[gateway_system.GetName]星系星门的能源！虽然这装置年代久远，但还是形成了一个完全可通过的亚空间通路。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:492`

### English

Our scientists have successfully restored power to the ancient Gateway in the [gateway_system.GetName] system! Despite its age, the device has formed a stable subspace conduit that is fully traversable.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:538`

## apoc.10.desc.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.10.desc.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们送去的探测器确认，我们激活此星门的行为也为第二座设施提供了能源。现在，这两个连接的星门是我们当前仅有的两个可以使用的设施，但是很可能在别的地方找到其他的处于休眠状态的设施。将来我们甚至能够在选定的星系重新建造全新的星门。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:493`

### English

The probes we sent through have confirmed that our activation of this Gateway has restored power to a second, identical device. For now, these two linked Gateways are the only ones that are accessible to us, but more inert devices can likely be found elsewhere. In the future, we may even be able to construct entirely new Gateways in systems of our choosing!

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:539`

## apoc.10.desc.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.10.desc.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发射的探测器确认此星门与整个网络的连接十分稳定。我们的舰船可以通过它旅行到其他激活的星门，只要星门的拥有者对我们不敌对或是对我们关闭了边境就没问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:494`

### English

The probes we have sent through confirm that this Gateway has achieved a strong link to the rest of the network. Our ships can now travel through it to other active Gateways, as long as their owners aren't hostile or have closed their borders to us.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:540`

## apoc.10.desc.3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.10.desc.3&lang=en)

### 中文

$apoc.10.desc.d$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:495`

### English

$apoc.10.desc.d$

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:541`

## apoc.10.desc.4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.10.desc.4&lang=en)

### 中文

$apoc.10.desc.d$\n\n$apoc.10.desc.e$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:496`

### English

$apoc.10.desc.d$\n\n$apoc.10.desc.e$

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:542`

## apoc.10.desc.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.10.desc.d&lang=en)

### 中文

位于§H[gateway_system.GetName]§!星系的被遗弃的传送门突然被激活了！很可能是其他地方的星门将此传送门的能源恢复了，现在此星门已经形成了一个稳定的亚空间回路，应该可以进行传送了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:497`

### English

The abandoned Gateway in the §H[gateway_system.GetName]§! system has suddenly been reactivated! Power must have been remotely restored to it somehow, possibly from another Gateway. It has formed a stable subspace conduit that appears traversable.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:543`

## apoc.10.desc.e

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.10.desc.e&lang=en)

### 中文

但是，我们无法进入星门，星门周围的亚空间力场会推开任何接近的舰船。在我们可以使用这个星门之前，我们必须研究从我们这侧激活星门的方法，这样它才能允许我们的舰船通行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:498`

### English

Unfortunately, the Gateway does not allow us access. The subspace field surrounding the device pushes our ships away whenever they approach. Before we can make use of it, we will have to research some way of activating the Gateway from our side so that it allows our ships to pass.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:544`

## apoc.10.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.10.a&lang=en)

### 中文

随时都可能有东西从那里出来！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:499`

### English

Something might come through at any time!

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:545`

## apoc.11.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.11.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星门建设完成

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:500`

### English

Gateway Constructed

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:546`

## apoc.11.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.11.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[gateway_system.GetName]恒星系完成了我们第一个可以投入使用的星门，这可以被称为是科技和工程的大胜利！星门已形成了稳定的亚空间通路并与星系的其他星门网络成功建立了链接。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:501`

### English

In what can only be described as a triumph of science and engineering, we have finished construction of our first operational Gateway in the [gateway_system.GetName] system! The device has formed a stable subspace conduit and established a successful link with the rest of the galaxy's Gateway network.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:547`

## apoc.12.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.12.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在§H[gateway_system.GetName]§!星系内发现的星门已经恢复供电。它已与星门网络其他部分建立连接，处于正常运转状态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:502`

### English

Power has been restored to the derelict Gateway that was found in the §H[gateway_system.GetName]§! system. It has been connected to the rest of the Gateway network and is now fully operational.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:548`

## apoc.13.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.13.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

位于§H[gateway_system.GetName]§!星系内的新星门已经建造完毕。它已与其余传送门网络建立稳定的亚空间连接，并且正常运转。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:503`

### English

Construction has concluded on the new Gateway in the §H[gateway_system.GetName]§! system. A stable subspace connection to the rest of the Gateway network has been established, and it is now fully operational.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:549`

## apoc.14.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.14.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星门基址就绪

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:504`

### English

Gateway Site Ready

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:550`

## apoc.14.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.14.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[gateway_system.GetName]§!星系中为建造星门而准备的深空建造基址已经准备就绪。一旦我们的必要资源准备完毕，星门的正式建造随时都可以动工。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:505`

### English

The deep space construction site for the planned Gateway in the §H[gateway_system.GetName]§! system is now in place. Once we have the necessary resources, construction of the actual Gateway may commence at any time.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:551`

## planet_destruction.111.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.111.name&lang=en)

### 中文

超空间捷径

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:506`

### English

Hyperspace Bypass

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:554`

## planet_destruction.111.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.111.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

超空间规划委员会报告，将太阳系的第三行星从重力井移除后，一个天然虫洞形成了。这个虫洞也许能逐渐缓解附近超空间通路的超光速交通拥堵。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:507`

### English

The Hyperspace Planning Council reports that the removal of the third planet in Sol from the system's gravity well has allowed a natural wormhole to form. This wormhole may eventually reduce FTL traffic congestion in the nearby hyperlanes.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:555`

## planet_destruction.111.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.111.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这该死的行星，我完全没有同情它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:508`

### English

Apathetic bloody planet, I've no sympathy at all.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:556`

## planet_destruction.111.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.111.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[sol_sys.GetName]§!里已形成一个§H$NATURAL_WORMHOLE_GALACTIC$§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:509`

### English

A §H$NATURAL_WORMHOLE_GALACTIC$§! has formed in §Y[sol_sys.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:557`

## planet_destruction.210.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.210.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]被屏蔽

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:510`

### English

[From.GetName] Shielded

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:558`

## planet_destruction.210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

从[From.From.GetName]发出的耀眼光束逐渐暗淡，[From.GetName]寂静无声。\n\n也许历史会将这次屏蔽，这次将[From.GetName]从大宇宙完全隔离的行为，视作极端且不合理。也许也不会。不管如何，从现在起[From.GetStarName]星系有了一颗行星规模的水晶球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:511`

### English

The glittering beam from the [From.From.GetName] fades, and [From.GetName] is silent.\n\nPerhaps history will call the shielding, this complete severing of [From.GetName] from the greater cosmos, extreme and unjustified. Or perhaps not. For now, the [From.GetStarName] system has a new planetary terrarium.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:559`

## planet_destruction.210.desc.alt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.210.desc.alt&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.GetName]炫目的光芒已渐渐黯淡，[From.GetName]归于沉寂。\n\n生活将在封闭的世界中继续。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:512`

### English

The glittering beam from the [From.From.GetName] fades, and [From.GetName] is silent.\n\nLife goes on, if contained.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:560`

## crisis.52.2.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrisis.52.2.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

很多哨兵都有军旅背景。我们都曾是与索林原虫战斗的各个舰队的幸存者。虽然曾经战败，但我们汲取了过去的经验与教训，并争取不再重蹈覆辙。\n\n就像之前所说过的，哨兵队的成员来自星系的各个物种。我们的希望寄托在一小队已不在此星系出现的失落帝国幸存者身上——他们向我们援助秘密科技和封存的舰队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:517`

### English

Many Sentinels have military backgrounds. We are survivors from a dozen different fleets that fought the Prethoryn and lost. As Sentinels, we have learned from the mistakes of the past and work hard never to repeat them.\n\nAlso, I believe I mentioned that we recruit from all species in the galaxy. We count among our numbers a handful of scattered survivors from an ancient fallen empire that no longer maintains a presence in this galaxy. They have given us access to secret technology caches and mothballed warfleets.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:566`

## crisis.52.new.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrisis.52.new.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经在§H[sentinel_system.GetName]§!附近建立了总部。这里曾经是一个未被标明的黑洞。是§H[sentinel_fallen_empire.GetName]§!将这个地点告诉我们的。他们还协助建设了哨兵阿尔法——我们首座空间设施，也是我们舰队的母港。\n\n这可以说是一项标志性的成就，尤其是在我们的时间如此紧迫的条件下。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:518`

### English

We have established our headquarters near §H[sentinel_system.GetName]§!. The coordinates of this previously uncharted black hole were given to us by the §H[sentinel_fallen_empire.GetName]§!. They also aided in the construction of Sentinel Alpha, our primary space installation and the home port of our fleets.\n\nIt is quite a remarkable achievement, if I may say so. Especially given the limited time we had.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:567`

## crisis.52.2.new.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrisis.52.2.new.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经在§H[sentinel_system.GetName]§!附近建立了总部。这里曾经是一个未被标明的黑洞。我们通过一个已经消失的失落帝国的数据库恢复了该黑洞的位置。在此数据库中，我们还获得了一些技术图纸，并在哨兵阿尔法的建设中派上了用场。这是我们首座空间设施，也是我们舰队的母港。\n\n这可以说是一项标志性的成就，尤其是在我们的时间如此紧迫的条件下。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:519`

### English

We have established our headquarters near §H[sentinel_system.GetName]§!. The coordinates of this uncharted black hole were recovered by our agents from the ancient databanks of a fallen empire now lost to history. From this databank we also obtained technical schematics that aided in the construction of Sentinel Alpha, our primary space installation and the home port of our fleets.\n\nIt is quite a remarkable achievement, if I may say so. Especially given the limited time we had.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:568`

## sentinel_title

### 中文

哨兵组织

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:520`

### English

The Sentinel Order

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:569`

## crisis.76.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrisis.76.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

历经艰难险阻，我们星系终于获得了胜利，击败了索林原虫。曾经，我还担心这一天永远不会到来。但现在，我就只剩下最后一项任务了。\n\n身为哨兵组织的大团长，我特此宣布解散此组织。我们唯一的目的就是击败索林原虫。既然此目标已经达成，那就该是我们分道扬镳的时候了。为了防止我们现有的战舰遭到滥用，这些战舰将被拆解。\n\n愿此次星河浩劫的结束标志着和平的新纪元的开始。这里是哨兵一号，通信结束。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:521`

### English

Against all odds, our galaxy has prevailed. The Prethoryn have been vanquished. I feared this day would never come, but now that victory is here, one last task remains for me.\n\nAs the current Grand Master of the Sentinels, I hereby disband our order. Our sole purpose was to see to the defeat of the Scourge, and now that this has been accomplished, it is time for our members to go their separate ways. To prevent their misuse, our surviving battle fleets will be dismantled.\n\nMay the defeat of this intergalactic menace herald a new era of peace. This is Sentinel One, signing off.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:570`

哨兵的末路

### English

## crisis.81.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrisis.81.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

据报告，§H[sentinel_system.GetName]§!附近出现激烈的战斗。哨兵组织的母星系处于崩溃的边缘。\n\n我们收到了一段从§H哨兵阿尔法§!——哨兵组织用作总部的军用空间站，传来的长程通讯片段。该恒星系因大量武器发射和能量残余造成了严重的信号干扰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:524`

### English

Intense fighting has been reported in the immediate vicinity of §H[sentinel_system.GetName]§!. The home system of the Sentinels is on the verge of being overrun.\n\nWe have picked up a partial long-range transmission from §HSentinel Alpha§!, the battlestation that serves as the headquarters of the Sentinel Order. Jamming and residual energy pockets from the massive amount of weapon discharges now occurring within the system are causing heavy signal interference.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:573`

## crisis.82.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrisis.82.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这里……这里是哨兵阿尔法。我们遭遇§R###信号损坏###§!……致命损伤。舰体已经凹陷，并且§R###信号损坏###§!……团长已确认牺牲。敌人数量实在太多！\n\n我们……已经完蛋了。不要放弃。为了我们的未来，你们绝对不能投降。不要让我们的努力白费！我们……我们……啊啊啊啊啊啊啊啊啊——§R<信号丢失>§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:525`

### English

This... this is Sentinel Alpha. We have §R###SIGNAL CORRUPTION###§! ...sustained critical damage. The hull is buckling, and §R###SIGNAL CORRUPTION###§! ...Grand Master confirmed dead. There are simply too many of them!\n\nIt's... it's over for us. Keep up the fight. For the sake of future generations, you must not yield. Do not let our sacrifice be in vain! We have... we... AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHH---- §R<SIGNAL LOST>§!

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:574`

## crisis.78.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrisis.78.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这里是哨兵一号。星系已经到了危难时刻，但我还是要告诉你们一些好消息。\n\n通过在漫长的战斗中吸取到的经验，以及艰苦的研究，我们的特工终于编制出来了极为详尽的虫群天灾生物的解剖资料。例如，你们是否知道索林原虫幼虫在主胆囊和副胆囊同时被刺穿的情况下就会剧烈燃烧？\n\n我们要将这些文件公之于众。你们可以瞄准我们发现的这些弱点进行攻击，这样你们的飞船就能够更加高效地进行战斗。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:526`

### English

This is Sentinel One. These are truly desperate times for the galaxy, but I am here to bring you some good news.\n\nThrough extensive combat experience and painstaking research, Sentinel agents have compiled extremely detailed anatomy files on the creatures of the Prethoryn Scourge. For example, did you know that Prethoryn Swarmlings have been known to violently combust if both their primary and secondary gallbladders are punctured at the same time?\n\nWe are making these files available to everyone in the galaxy. By targeting the weaknesses we have found, you should find that your fleets are much more effective in battle against the Scourge.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:575`

## crisis.78.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrisis.78.c&lang=en)

### 中文

将这些资料上传至瞄准计算机。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:527`

### English

Upload this data to our targeting computers.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:576`

## sentinel_data_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asentinel_data_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些索林原虫的具体解剖资料是哨兵组织给予我们的。有了这些资料的帮助，我们已能在虫群生物的外骨骼上辨识出数处弱点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:529`

### English

These detailed anatomy files on the Prethoryn were given to us by the Sentinels. With their help we have been able to identify several weak points in the exoskeletons of the Prethoryn creatures.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:578`

## crisis.121.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrisis.121.name&lang=en)

### 中文

发现野生索林原虫

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:530`

### English

Feral Prethoryn Sighted

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:579`

## crisis.121.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrisis.121.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在虫群天灾被击败后，我们未能掌握一些趁乱逃跑地索林原虫的踪迹。没有中枢主巢的指引，这些没有心智的野兽大部分会凋谢消散。\n\n然而，最近有目击报告确认，一大群野生索林原虫在§H[feral_prethoryn_system.GetName]§!星系建立了一个新巢穴。\n\n在今后，我们有很大可能在虫灾曾经肆虐过的无人占领星系遇见野生虫群巢穴。不过它们不太可能发展成一个大威胁——这些野生生物易于陷入内斗，也没有表现出发展智力并继续演变为虫灾的迹象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:531`

### English

Many Prethoryn creatures remain unaccounted for after the defeat of the Scourge, having slipped away into the depths of space in the ensuing chaos. With no central hive consciousness to guide them, most of these mindless beasts will eventually wither and perish.\n\nHowever, recent sightings have confirmed that a large number of these feral Prethoryn have established a new lair in the §H[feral_prethoryn_system.GetName]§! system.\n\nIn the coming years, we will likely face the possibility of more feral Prethoryn nesting in unoccupied systems where the Scourge once had a presence. They are unlikely to develop into a major threat - these wild creatures are prone to infighting and do not exhibit the calculating intelligence that drove the Scourge onwards.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:580`

### English

## sentinel_fleet_gift

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asentinel_fleet_gift&lang=en)

### 中文

一艘哨兵舰船出现在[Root.Capital.GetName]附近，并交由你控制。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:534`

### English

A Sentinel fleet appears near [Root.Capital.GetName] and is transferred over to your control.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:583`

## pirate.6.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apirate.6.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一小群拥有一种罕见基因突变的子个体，可使它们切断与蜂巢思维的部分连接而不会陷入休眠。不过如果离开蜂巢思维太远，这些离群的子个体就会死亡，所以它们盘踞在了位于我们境内§H[pirate_system.GetName]§!星系。\n\n它们打算当海盗来维持自身，利用少数简陋的飞船打劫穿越该恒星系不设防的贸易路线。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:535`

### English

A rare genetic mutation present in a small number of drones has allowed them to partly sever their connection to the Mind without being rendered comatose. As these rogue drones would perish if they ventured too far from the Mind's influence, they have established themselves in the §H[pirate_system.GetName]§! system, within our space.\n\nFrom there they intend to sustain themselves by living as pirates, using their small fleet of crude spacecraft to raid the vulnerable trade route that passes through that system.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:585`

## pirate.7.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apirate.7.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一小群故障的单元已经脱离了[Root.GetName]的中枢，并躲进了我们境内的§H[pirate_system.GetName]§!星系。这些单位已经无可挽回地腐化，并开始利用简陋的舰船劫掠穿越该恒星系不设防的贸易路线，掠夺零件和资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:536`

### English

A small group of malfunctioning units have separated themselves from the main intelligence of the [Root.GetName], and sought refuge in the §H[pirate_system.GetName]§! system, inside our space. These units have become irredeemably corrupted, and they now use crude spacecraft to raid the vulnerable trade route that passes through the system for spare parts and resources.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:586`

## pirate.50.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apirate.50.name&lang=en)

### 中文

发现海盗！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:537`

### English

Pirates Sighted!

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:587`

## pirate.50.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apirate.50.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们位于§H[pirate_system.GetName]§!星系的贸易路线没有得到很好的保护，现在近乎变成了法外之地。该恒星系已经吸引了越来越多的海盗、走私贩以及其它各种不法分子，肆无忌惮地掠夺我们的货物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:538`

### English

Our trade route in the §H[pirate_system.GetName]§! system is poorly protected and passes through what has largely become lawless space. An increasing number of pirates, smugglers and various other criminal elements have been drawn to the system, and they now raid our shipping with near impunity.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:588`

## pirate.50.b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apirate.50.b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

途经§H[pirate_system.GetName]§!星系的无防备贸易路线已经引起了一群海盗们的注意。这些匪徒集结了一支大舰队，拦截了该恒星系的大部分贸易。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:539`

### English

The unprotected trade route that passes through the §H[pirate_system.GetName]§! system has attracted the attention of a roving band of pirates. These outlaws have gathered a sizeable fleet that is intercepting much of the system's trade.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:589`

## pirate.50.c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apirate.50.c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

因其贸易路线毫无防备，§H[pirate_system.GetName]§!星系已经吸引了大批不法分子，并且在最近的一次权力争夺之后，这些犯罪分子们已经有了足够的组织性，组成了一支大海盗舰队。他们利用这支东拼西凑的舰队疯狂地劫掠恒星系内的贸易船。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:540`

### English

With its vulnerable and unprotected trade route, the §H[pirate_system.GetName]§! system has attracted a large number of ne'er-do-wells, and following a recent power struggle, these criminal degenerates are now sufficiently organized to operate a significant pirate armada. They are using this ragtag fleet to intercept as much trade in the system as they can.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:590`

## pirate.50.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apirate.50.a&lang=en)

### 中文

他们必须被正法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:541`

### English

They must be dealt with.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:591`

## pirate.53.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apirate.53.name&lang=en)

### 中文

反叛子个体在劫掠

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:542`

### English

Rogue Drone Raiding

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:592`

## pirate.53.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apirate.53.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一小群子个体上出现了罕见的基因突变，可使它们切断与蜂巢思维的部分连接而不会陷入休眠。不过如果离开蜂巢思维太远，这些离群的子个体就会死亡，所以它们盘踞在了位于我们境内的§H[pirate_system.GetName]§!星系。\n\n它们并不是碰巧选中的这个恒星系，而是因为我们一支最不设防的贸易路线正好途经该恒星系。那些离群的子个体无疑打算当海盗来维持生计，利用它们的少数简陋飞船打劫穿越该恒星系不设防的贸易路线。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:543`

### English

A rare genetic mutation present in a small number of drones has allowed them to partly sever their connection to the Mind without being rendered comatose. As these rogue drones would perish if they ventured too far from the Mind's influence, they have established themselves in the §H[pirate_system.GetName]§! system within our borders.\n\nThis system was not chosen at random - one of our most vulnerable trade routes passes through it, and the rogue drones no doubt intend to sustain themselves by living as pirates, using their small fleet of crude spacecraft to raid our shipping.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:593`

## pirate.54.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apirate.54.name&lang=en)

### 中文

外星海盗

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:544`

### English

Alien Pirates

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:594`

## pirate.54.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apirate.54.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一群外星海盗已经潜入并盘踞在了位于我们境内的§H[pirate_system.GetName]§!星系。我们有一条不设防的贸易路线途经该恒星系，它们似乎打算掠夺这条贸易路线来维持自己的犯罪活动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:545`

### English

A group of alien pirates have infiltrated and established themselves in the §H[pirate_system.GetName]§! system, inside our space. To fund their criminal activities, they appear intent on raiding our unprotected trade route that passes through that system.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:595`

## pirate.56.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apirate.56.name&lang=en)

### 中文

失控单元

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:546`

### English

Corrupted Units

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:596`

## pirate.56.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apirate.56.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一小群故障的单位已经脱离了[Root.GetName]的主体智能，并躲进了我们境内的§H[pirate_system.GetName]§!星系。这些单位已经无可救药，并开始利用简陋的舰船打劫穿越该恒星系不设防的贸易路线，掠夺零件和资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:547`

### English

A small group of malfunctioning units have separated themselves from the main intelligence of the [Root.GetName], and sought refuge in the §H[pirate_system.GetName]§! system inside our space. These units have become irredeemably corrupted, and they now use crude spacecraft to raid the system's unprotected trade route for spare parts and resources.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:597`

## pirate.57.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apirate.57.name&lang=en)

### 中文

有机生物海盗

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:548`

### English

Organic Pirates

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:598`

## pirate.57.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apirate.57.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一小群有机生物体的犯罪分子已经设法潜入并盘踞于我们境内的§H[pirate_system.GetName]§!星系。他们似乎打算打劫途经该恒星系不设防的贸易路线，或许是心存有机生物常见的侥幸心理，误以为他们的存在无关紧要，不会令我们动用武力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:549`

### English

A small group of organic sub-units, apparently criminal outcasts, have somehow infiltrated and established themselves in the §H[pirate_system.GetName]§! system inside our borders. They seem intent on raiding the system's undefended trade route, perhaps gambling, as organics are wont to do, on the mistaken notion that they are too insignificant to warrant an armed response from us.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:599`

## leviathans.509.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.509.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

根据我们当初签订的协定，你们对我们数据库的使用期限已经到了。现在你们打算让使用期限延长十年呢？还是不再需要我们数据库的信息了呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:550`

### English

The agreement we signed giving you access to our databanks has run its course. Would you like to extend it for another ten standard years, or do your researchers no longer require the information we offer?

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:601`

## leviathans.509.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.509.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们愿意延长协议。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:551`

### English

We would like to extend our deal.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:602`

## leviathans.509.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.509.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

很好，你们的科学家可以继续使用我们的数据库。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:552`

### English

Excellent. Our databanks shall remain available to your scientists.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:603`

## leviathans.509.e

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.509.e&lang=en)

### 中文

不，这次就不用了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:553`

### English

No, we are not interested at this time.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:604`

## enclave_mineral_trade_1_xur

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave_mineral_trade_1_xur&lang=en)

### 中文

雪拉企业贸易协定

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:554`

### English

XuraCorp Trade Deal

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:605`

## enclave_mineral_trade_1_xur_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave_mineral_trade_1_xur_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这项贸易协定将在我方和雪拉企业间指定每月资源交换。\n\n§Y你可以在和雪拉企业的外交界面随时终止此协定。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:555`

### English

This trade deal stipulates a monthly transfer of resources between us and XuraCorp.\n\n§YIt can be ended at any time in the XuraCorp diplomatic menu.§!

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:606`

## enclave_mineral_trade_1_rig_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave_mineral_trade_1_rig_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这项贸易协定将在我方和里甘之间指定每月资源交换。\n\n§Y你可以在和里甘的外交界面随时终止此协定。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:592`

### English

This trade deal stipulates a monthly transfer of resources between us and the Riggans.\n\n§YIt can be ended at any time in the Riggan diplomatic menu.§!

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:643`

## end_trade_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aend_trade_3&lang=en)

### 中文

这将结束我们目前与穆塔根进行的资源贸易协定。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:664`

### English

This will terminate our current resource trade deal with the Muutagans.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:715`

## leviathans.105.e

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.105.e&lang=en)

### 中文

我们希望结束资源贸易协定。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:665`

### English

We wish to end our resource trade deal.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:716`

## leviathans.105.e.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.105.e.response&lang=en)

### 中文

很好。我们会马上通知我们的货船，指示他们待命并返回母港。\n\n如果你改变心意的话请随时告诉我们！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:666`

### English

Very well. We shall immediately transmit instructions to our freighters telling them to stand down and return to base.\n\nLet us know if you change your mind!

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:717`

## leviathans.171.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.171.name&lang=en)

### 中文

能量贸易已取消

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:667`

### English

Energy Trade Canceled

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:718`

## leviathans.171.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.171.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们能源储备的消耗殆尽，以能源为支付手段的贸易已无法继续进行，因此所有与商人城邦之间的与能源相关协定都已取消。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:668`

### English

With our energy reserves depleted, we can no longer maintain our trade agreements that depended on this resource. All such agreements with Trader Enclaves have been canceled.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:719`

## leviathans.171.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.171.a&lang=en)

那可不妙。

### English

That is not good.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:720`

## decision_make_holy_world_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_make_holy_world_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在自己的领域内寻找一颗未被殖民的§Y$pc_gaia$§!作为圣地。只要它未被凡人玷污，并且仍处于我们境内，这颗神圣不可侵犯的星球将为我们提供：£unity£ §Y$country_base_unity_produces_mult$§!§G+10%§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:681`

### English

We search our domain for an uncolonized §Y$pc_gaia$§! to designate as a Holy World. As long as it remains unspoiled by mortals and within our borders, this sacrosanct planet will give us: £unity£ §Y$country_base_unity_produces_mult$§! §G+10%§!.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:735`

## apoc.200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapoc.200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在神学专家得协助下，几位占卜师已经得出结论——§Y[holy_target.GetName]§!是[holy_target.GetStarName]星系内值得人们倍加崇拜的行星。\n\n官方的认可肯定能够增加公众对这个神秘世界的尊崇。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:683`

### English

In cooperation with theological experts, several scryers have independently confirmed §Y[holy_target.GetName]§! in the [holy_target.GetStarName] system as being worthy of particular veneration.\n\nOfficial recognition is sure to boost the public's appreciation of this mystical world.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:738`

沃陶姆母星

### English

Vultaum Homeworld

## vultaum_homeworld_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avultaum_homeworld_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗星球是先驱者文明沃陶姆曾经的母星。虽然整颗行星已经支离破碎并且不可能再支持任何生命存在，但我们仍能在行星表面发现大片的城市废墟。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:708`

### English

The ancient homeworld of the Vultaum. Though the planet has been shattered and no longer supports life, the remnants of vast cities can still be found on the surface.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:771`

## vultaum_minerals

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avultaum_minerals&lang=en)

### 中文

轨道设施残骸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:709`

### English

Orbital Facility Debris

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:772`

## vultaum_minerals_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avultaum_minerals_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个沃陶姆的巨型轨道设施于千万年前坠毁在了这颗行星表面，使得行星表面布满了残骸和碎片。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:710`

### English

A massive Vultaum orbital facility of some kind crashed on this planet eons ago. The surface is littered with debris.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:773`

## vultaum_energy

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avultaum_energy&lang=en)

### 中文

浮空采矿网络

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:711`

### English

Aerostat Mining Network

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:774`

## vultaum_energy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avultaum_energy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗气态巨行星曾经支撑着一个巨大的浮空采矿设施网络，这个巨大网络是沃陶姆用来提取氦-3的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:712`

### English

This gas giant once supported a vast network of aerostat mining facilities that were used by the Vultaum to extract helium-3.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:775`

## yuht_homeworld

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ayuht_homeworld&lang=en)

### 中文

尤特母星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:713`

### English

Yuht Homeworld

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:776`

## yuht_homeworld_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ayuht_homeworld_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

先驱者文明尤特人就是发源于这颗行星。行星的表面已经被轨道轰炸摧毁，绝大部分建筑早已化为废墟，但仍有一些地下设施可供研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:714`

### English

The ancient Yuht originated on this planet. Orbital bombardment has pulverized most structures on the surface, but there are still pockets of abandoned subterranean habitats that could be studied.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:777`

## yuht_minerals

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ayuht_minerals&lang=en)

### 中文

资源丰富的小行星带

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:715`

### English

Rich Asteroid Belt

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:778`

## yuht_minerals_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ayuht_minerals_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个富矿的小行星带曾经被尤特人大规模开采，在这里采集到的矿物支持着尤特人星际帝国缓慢而又稳定的扩张。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:716`

### English

This rich asteroid belt was extensively mined by the Yuht, and the minerals found here supported the slow but steady expansion of their interstellar empire.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:779`

## yuht_energy

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ayuht_energy&lang=en)

### 中文

太阳能收集阵列

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:717`

### English

Solar Collector Arrays

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:780`

## yuht_energy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ayuht_energy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一些古老的太阳能收集阵列被尤特人部署在这颗气态巨行星的轨道上。令人难以置信的是，它们中的有些还能继续运转。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:718`

### English

Several ancient solar collector arrays were placed in orbit around this gas giant by the Yuht. Incredibly, some of them are still functional.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:781`

## first_league_homeworld

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_league_homeworld&lang=en)

### 中文

第一联盟首都星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:719`

### English

First League Capital

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:782`

## first_league_homeworld_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_league_homeworld_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这座行星规模的坟墓曾经是第一联盟伟大的行政中心。现在这颗行星已经成为了不能支持任何生命的不毛之地，但是完全覆盖行星表面的城市废墟依然随处可见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:720`

### English

This planetary mausoleum was once the shining administrative center of the First League. It no longer supports life, but ruins from the city that once stretched across the entire surface can be found everywhere.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:783`

## first_league_minerals

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_league_minerals&lang=en)

### 中文

废弃的采矿设施

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:721`

### English

Abandoned Mining Facilities

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:784`

## first_league_minerals_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_league_minerals_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个小行星带在第一联合鼎盛时期人烟浩穰。我们仍能在其中数千颗小行星上发现废弃的采矿设施。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:722`

### English

This asteroid belt was densely populated in the heyday of the First League. Abandoned mining facilities can be found on thousands of asteroids.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:785`

## first_league_energy

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_league_energy&lang=en)

### 中文

维修平台

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:723`

### English

Maintenance Platform

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:786`

## first_league_energy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_league_energy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个气态巨行星上建有几个巨大的维修平台，它们曾被用于维修第一联盟商舰队的货船。平台上的一些能源传输设备至今仍然能正常运行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:724`

### English

This gas giant was home to several colossal maintenance platforms that serviced the hundreds of thousands of freighters in the First League merchant marine. Some of the energy transfer equipment is still functional.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:787`

## irassian_homeworld

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Airassian_homeworld&lang=en)

### 中文

伊拉斯人母星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:725`

### English

Irassian Homeworld

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:788`

## irassian_homeworld_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Airassian_homeworld_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管遭到许多次轨道轰炸，伊拉斯人的母星上遗留的无数古器仍然是伊拉斯人那发达文明的象征。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:726`

### English

Despite later attempts to sterilize its surface through orbital bombardment, the ancient homeworld of the Irassians is still home to a tremendous amount of artifacts from their advanced civilization.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:789`

## irassian_minerals_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Airassian_minerals_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

此地曾经是伊拉斯人星际舰队的大型锚地，现在已化为一片废墟散落于星球轨道之上；这其中含有资源丰富的矿物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:728`

### English

Once a major anchorage of the Irassian Starfleet, the ruined naval yards that litter the orbit of this planet have deposited a wealth of mineral resources to its surface.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:791`

## irassian_energy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Airassian_energy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在伊拉斯协约国早期的星际扩张时期，这个气态巨行星是伊拉斯人的重要能源产地。行星轨道上的大片发电站至今仍然存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/apocalypse_l_simp_chinese.yml:730`

### English

This gas giant served as the key energy production site of the Irassian Concordat in the early stages of its interstellar expansion. A dense belt of ancient orbital power stations still ring the planet.

Source: `localisation/english/apocalypse_l_english.yml:793`
